We summarize the architecture of this prevention based distributed system's service called Service Health Checker, explain how check authors compose templated metric queries, thresholds, and workflow predicates; and discuss how the system is integrated with tiered and phased rollouts so that regressions trigger automatic rollback. We then describe the operational problems that emerged at scale, such as noise, alert fatigue, drift, and uncovered regressions, and the program of measurement, tooling, and improved defaults we deployed to address them. We close with lessons learned from years of operating deployment health checks at Meta, and the directions we are exploring next, including AI assisted health check tuning. Index Terms: deployment safety, continuous deployment, monitoring, software reliability, release engineering, software reliability engineering, AIOps, anomaly detection

</details>


### [271] [Testing and Evaluation of Agentic AI Systems In Military Command and Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20597)
*Ulysse Richard,Heather Frase,Sarah Cao,Di Cooke,Sebastian Kwon,Adrianna Tan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统审查了240项军事指挥控制（C2）场景中Agentic AI系统的测试与评估（T&E）实践，识别出已建立方法对测试对象做出的八大假设，这些假设被Agentic属性削弱，导致测试结果无法支持从测试行为到实战行为的推理。文章从三大假设簇推导出十项保证声明，并评估现有及新兴方法的应对能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 军事指挥控制（C2）领域正在采购Agentic AI系统，并公开承诺严格测试和人工监督。然而这些承诺能否兑现取决于其支撑性保证案例（assurance case），而现有评估方法对Agentic系统的适用性缺乏系统审查，导致测试结果可能满足流程要求却无法保障部署行为的安全性。

Method: 对240项已记录的测试与评估（T&E）实践进行结构化审查，覆盖8个评估维度和3个生命周期阶段。识别出既有方法对测试对象隐含的8项假设，将其归类为系统可指定性（specifiability）、稳定性（stability）、可组合性（composability）和可监督性（supervisability）四大聚类。从前三聚类推导出10项保证声明，并通过5个C2场景映射评估现有及新兴方法的能力。

Result: Agentic属性削弱了全部八项假设，但这种侵蚀影响的是连接证据与声明的论证环节，而非声明或证据本身。可监督性聚类未被评估，因其依赖于系统稳定性结果和人类因素T&E方法。记录表明无法支持系统级行为的大范围声明，但较窄的声明在原则上可恢复，包括有界任务包线、轨迹接地正确性、可执行的运行时约束和特征化的运行间方差。

Conclusion: 系统性保证的举证责任部分转移至部署阶段，使"部署决定"成为持续过程。在无法生成证据的地方，剩余不确定性可通过定义的过期条件和归属所有权来治理。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems are being procured for military command and control (C2) under public commitments to rigorous testing and human oversight. Whether such commitments can be discharged depends on their supporting assurance case, which requires three elements: claims specifying the conditions for acceptability, evidence bearing on those claims, and an argument connecting the two. Through a structured review of 240 documented Testing and Evaluation (T&E) practices, spanning eight evaluation dimensions and three lifecycle stages, we identify eight assumptions that established methods make about their test article, grouped into four clusters: system specifiability, stability, composability, and supervisability. Agentic properties weaken all eight assumptions. This erosion affects the argument connecting evidence to claims, not the claims or evidence themselves. As a result, test results may satisfy process requirements, but they do not warrant the inference from tested to fielded behavior.
  We derive ten assurance claims for the first three assumption clusters and assess whether current and emerging methods can address each, mapping operational consequences through five C2 scenarios. Supervisability is identified but not assessed here, since evidencing it depends on system stability results and human factors T&E methods beyond the present scope. The documented record does not support broad claims about system-level behavior, but narrower claims remain recoverable in principle, contingent on mature methods: bounded mission envelopes, trajectory-grounded correctness, executable runtime constraints, and characterized run-to-run variance. Part of the evidentiary burden shifts into deployment, making the determination to field a continuing act. Where evidence cannot be generated, the residual uncertainty can be governed through defined expiry conditions and assigned ownership.

</details>


### [272] [Behavior Specification-Guided Program Synthesis for Binary Deobfuscation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20628)
*Kangchen Zhu,Shangwen Wang,Zhiliang Tian,Zhouyang Jia,Xiaoling Li,Jun Ma,Jie Yu,Xiaoguang Mao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出BinMirror，一种将二进制反混淆从结构变换范式转向行为驱动综合的新方法。通过将动态执行轨迹和交互快照作为行为规格，BinMirror合成高质量源代码并对照运行时观察进行验证，在150万合成混淆二进制数据上实现了74.5%的单元测试Pass@1，显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有二进制反混淆研究主要采用"反编译-结构变换"范式，但由于编译过程丢失了高层语义（如精确类型信息和源代码级结构），导致生成代码质量低且无法保证恢复代码保持原始程序的运行时行为。源码头反混淆研究较多，但二进制级别的反混淆却鲜有探索。

Method: 提出范式转变：从结构变换转向行为驱动的综合。核心洞察是混淆虽扭曲程序内部结构，但语义保持变换必须保留程序的可观察执行行为。BinMirror将二进制反混淆重构为行为规格引导的程序综合任务，以动态执行轨迹和交互快照作为行为规格，综合高质量源代码并对照从高度混淆二进制收集的运行时观察进行验证。

Result: 在150万个合成混淆二进制上进行了广泛评估，BinMirror显著优于最先进的基线方法，在极端混淆条件下实现了74.5%的单元测试Pass@1。

Conclusion: 实验结果证明了BinMirror在真实世界安全分析中恢复语义清晰度的实用价值，为二进制反混淆提供了新的研究范式。

Abstract: Deobfuscation is critical to reverse engineering and security analysis because it restores the readability and analyzability of obfuscated code. However, existing research primarily focuses on source-code deobfuscation, while binary-level deobfuscation remains largely underexplored despite its practical importance when source code is unavailable. Existing binary deobfuscation methods typically decompile binaries into pseudocode and then apply structural transformations. However, because compilation discards high-level semantics such as precise type information and source-level structures, this decompilation-based paradigm often produces low-quality code and provides limited assurance that the recovered code preserves the runtime behavior of the original program. To address these limitations, we propose a paradigm shift from structural transformation to behavior-driven synthesis. Our core insight is that although obfuscation distorts a program's internal structure, semantics-preserving transformations must retain its observable execution behavior. Based on this insight, we introduce BinMirror, an approach that reformulates binary deobfuscation as a behavior-specification-guided program synthesis task. By treating dynamic execution traces and interaction snapshots as behavioral specifications, BinMirror synthesizes high-quality source code and validates it against runtime observations collected from heavily obfuscated binaries. Extensive evaluations on 1.5 million synthetically obfuscated binaries show that BinMirror significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, achieving a unit-test Pass@1 of 74.5% under extreme obfuscation. These results demonstrate the practical utility of BinMirror in restoring semantic clarity for real-world security analysis.

</details>


### [273] [Toward Understanding Operating System Defects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20643)
*Hongyao Zuo,Jiali Li,Jiajun Jiang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对Android、Linux和HarmonyOS三个代表性操作系统中的1,500个缺陷进行了大规模实证研究，从缺陷发生层、影响功能、触发方式、严重程度和修复代码元素等多个维度进行系统分析，并进行了跨OS对比，提出了提升OS缺陷检测与调试的可行建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 操作系统是所有软件系统的基础，其缺陷可能导致系统崩溃和数据损坏，影响数十亿用户。理解OS缺陷的特征是设计有效缺陷检测和调试方法的基础，但目前缺乏足够大规模和全面深入的OS缺陷特征研究。

Method: 从Android、Linux和HarmonyOS三个覆盖移动端和桌面端的代表性操作系统中收集1,500个缺陷，从多个分类维度（OS层级、影响功能、触发方式、严重程度、修复涉及代码元素）进行分布分析，并进行维度间联合分析和跨OS相似性比较。

Result: 这是迄今为止该领域最大规模的研究。通过多维度分析和跨系统对比，总结出多项关于OS缺陷特征的重要发现，为OS质量保障提供了全面的理解基础。

Conclusion: 基于研究发现，提出了一系列可操作的改进建议，用于提升OS缺陷检测和调试的效果，为未来OS质量保障研究提供了指导方向。

Abstract: Operating systems (OS) serve as the foundation for all other software systems, and thus defects in OSes can lead to severe conquences, such as system crashes and data corruption, affecting billions of users. This broad impact underscores the necessity and importance of ensuring OS quality. Understanding the characteristics of OS defects is a fundamental step in this quality assurance task, as it facilitates the design of effective defect detection and debugging approaches. In this work, we conduct a large-scale study of 1,500 defects from three distinct and representative operating systems (Android, Linux, and HarmonyOS) spanning both mobile and desktop environments. To the best of our knowledge, this is the largest study of its kind in this domain. By analyzing the distribution of OS defects across multiple classification dimensions, including the OS layer where defects occur, the functions they affect, how they are triggered, their severity, and the code elements involved in their repair, as well as performing joint analysis between dimensions and cross-OS similarity comparisons, we summarize several major findings that contribute to a comprehensive understanding of OS defects across systems. Based on these findings, we provide a series of actionable implications for better OS defect detection and debugging, offering guidelines for future research aimed at improving OS quality assurance.

</details>


### [274] [Temporal Validity on Real Software Histories: Eliminating Stale-Fact Errors in Code-Assistant Memory over GitHub Fixes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20685)
*Neeraj Yadav*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: MemStrata在真实软件历史上验证了确定性（主体、关系、对象）超替记忆机制，从707个真实GitHub issues中提取130个干净的原子状态转换，达到0.91的答案准确率（RAG仅0.57-0.59），并将RAG提供过时值的情况从36-38%降至接近0，同时保持RAG级别的检索延迟（约2.1秒）


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG缺乏时间模型，当事实在编程会话中发生变化（函数重命名、端点移动、依赖升级）时，新旧值相似度极高，RAG无法区分哪个是最新的，导致返回过时的值。此前该机制仅在合成单值基准上验证过，需要端到端验证其在实际软件历史中的有效性。

Method: 从707个真实GitHub issues（SWE-bench Lite + Verified）中提取130个干净的原子状态转换，使每个样本无标记化（过时与当前语句仅在值上不同）。使用MemStrata的确定性超替记忆机制对比RAG（含LLM重排序器）进行测试。

Result: MemStrata达到0.91的答案准确率，而RAG仅0.57-0.59；当被强制回答时，RAG在36-38%的情况下提供超替值（LLM重排序器无帮助），MemStrata将其降至约0，且检索延迟与RAG相当（约2.1秒，重排序器约18秒）。研究中还发现并修复了一个真实的product bug（大小写/标点不敏感的值比较）。

Conclusion: MemStrata通过确定性超替记忆在真实代码变更历史中有效解决了RAG的时间感知缺陷，且不影响延迟。但值得注意其适用范围有限（仅约18%的真实修复是干净的原子转换），其余修复类型的提取覆盖是后续工作方向。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) has no model of time: when a fact changes across a coding session - a function is renamed, an endpoint moves, a dependency is bumped - RAG retrieves both the old and new value with near-identical similarity and cannot tell which is current, so it serves the superseded value. Paper 1 showed, on synthetic single-value benchmarks, that a deterministic (subject, relation, object) supersession memory eliminates this failure. Here we validate it end-to-end on real software history. From 707 real GitHub issues (SWE-bench Lite + Verified) we extract 130 clean atomic state transitions, a fix that changes one identifiable value from a pre-fix to a post-fix form, and render each marker-free (the stale and current statements differ only in the value). On this set, MemStrata reaches 0.91 answer accuracy versus RAG's 0.57-0.59; and, the structural result, when forced to answer RAG serves the superseded value 36-38% of the time (an LLM reranker does not help) while MemStrata drives this to ~0, at RAG retrieval latency (~2.1 s vs ~18 s for the reranker). We are explicit about scope: only ~18% of real fixes are clean atomic transitions; Paper 2 isolates the memory mechanism on that class, and extraction coverage of the remaining fixes is the orthogonal problem we defer to follow-on work. A real product bug surfaced and was fixed during the study (a case/punctuation-insensitive value comparison), with the moat property (deterministic-supersession accuracy on clean code mutations) preserved and verified.

</details>


### [275] [An Extensive Empirical Study on Code Translation Technique](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20776)
*Ruihang Fan,Jiajun Jiang,Xinpeng Wang,Jiateng Fu,Fengjie Li,Jiasi Shen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模实证研究，系统比较了基于学习的方法、基于大语言模型（LLM）的方法和通用LLM在方法级和类级代码翻译上的表现，分析正确性、相似度、翻译方向、翻译粒度及失败模式，发现LLM类方法在方法级正确性上优于学习类方法，类级翻译难度显著更高，静态语义错误和逻辑错误是主要挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化代码翻译对软件演化日益重要，但基于学习的方法与基于大语言模型的方法在代码翻译中的相对优势和局限性尚未被充分理解，缺乏系统性的实证对比研究。

Method: 开展大规模实证研究，在多种编程语言的方法级和类级基准上，对比评估学习类方法、LLM类方法以及通用LLM，分析维度包括可执行正确性、代码相似度、翻译方向、翻译粒度和失败模式。

Result: LLM和LLM类方法在方法级正确性上总体优于学习类方法；相似度指标不能可靠反映功能正确性；翻译方向显著影响性能，尤其在类型系统差异较大的语言之间；类级翻译比方法级翻译困难得多，需保持全局语义、接口、成员关系和跨方法依赖；静态语义错误和逻辑错误是主要失败原因。

Conclusion: 研究结果为开发更健壮、更具类型感知、结构感知和上下文感知的代码翻译技术提供了实证依据和实践指导。

Abstract: Automated code translation is increasingly important for software evolution, yet the relative strengths and limitations of learning-based and large language model (LLM)-based techniques remain insufficiently understood. To address this gap, we conduct a large-scale empirical study comparing representative code translation techniques across methodological paradigms and translation granularities. We evaluate learning-based methods, LLM-based methods, and general-purpose LLMs on multilingual method-level and class-level benchmarks involving multiple programming languages. Our analysis considers executable correctness, code similarity, translation direction, translation granularity, and failure patterns. The results show that LLMs and LLM-based methods generally outperform learning-based methods in method-level correctness, although similarity metrics alone do not reliably reflect functional correctness. Translation direction substantially affects performance, particularly when translating between languages with different type-system characteristics. Class-level translation remains considerably more difficult than method-level translation because it requires preserving global semantics, interfaces, member relationships, and cross-method dependencies. Our error analysis further shows that static semantic errors and logical errors are the primary challenges in existing code translation systems. These findings provide empirical evidence and practical guidance for developing more robust, type-aware, structure-aware, and context-aware code translation techniques.

</details>


### [276] [BC-Bench: Evaluating Agentic Engineering in a Domain-Specific Language for ERP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20851)
*Haoran Sun,Klaus Marius Hansen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出BC-Bench，一个针对Microsoft Dynamics 365 Business Central中AL领域特定语言的智能体工程基准测试，包含101个来自生产仓库的真实ERP任务，评估结果表明通用基准上的性能提升并不能一致迁移到AL领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用基准测试上智能体工程系统表现优异，但在ERP领域特定语言（DSL）上的有效性尚未被充分探索。AL生态系统面临公共资源有限和环境配置复杂等独特约束，需要专门的评测基准。

Method: 基于SWE-Bench方法论构建BC-Bench基准，从两个微软拥有的生产代码仓库中手工整理101个真实任务，涵盖代码生成和测试生成，支持多模态问题陈述（含视觉上下文），并使用多个前沿模型在两个智能体框架上通过多轮运行指标进行评估。

Result: 在Bug Fixing类别下，模型之间的解析率差异大于两个智能体框架之间的差异；通用基准上报告的改进并未一致地转移到AL领域。

Conclusion: 研究结果强调了领域特定评估的必要性，说明通用基准的性能提升不能直接推断到特定DSL环境中的实际表现。

Abstract: Agentic engineering systems have shown strong performance on general-purpose benchmarks, yet their effectiveness in enterprise resource planning (ERP) domain-specific languages (DSLs) remains underexplored. We introduce BC-Bench, a benchmark designed to evaluate agentic engineering on real-world tasks in AL, the DSL for Microsoft Dynamics 365 Business Central. BC-Bench comprises 101 manually curated tasks extracted from two Microsoft-owned production repositories, reflecting authentic ERP development workflows. Adapting the SWE-Bench methodology, we address the unique constraints of the AL ecosystem---including limited public resources and complex environment provisioning. Beyond generating functional code, BC-Bench evaluates test generation and supports multimodal problem statements where visual context is commonly present. We evaluate multiple frontier models across two agent harnesses, utilizing multi-run metrics to account for nondeterminism. In the Bug Fixing category, under our evaluated settings, between-model differences in resolution rate are larger than differences between the two evaluated agent harnesses, and improvements reported on general-purpose benchmarks do not consistently transfer to AL. These results highlight the need for domain-specific evaluation.

</details>


### [277] [Beyond the Traceback: Using LLMs for Adaptive Explanations of Programming Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20896)
*Alexandru-Radu Moraru,Shreyan Biswas,Ujwal Gadiraju*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过N=103的多阶段众包实验，评估了面向不同技能水平的LLM生成的Python错误消息。结果显示，LLM重写的错误信息虽然显著提升了主观评价（如可读性和认知负荷），但并未带来客观调试性能的显著提升，揭示了人-AI互补性差距：感觉更好的解释未必让用户成为更有效的调试者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编程错误信息对新手开发者至关重要但难以理解，虽然LLM可以重写错误信息使其更清晰，但缺乏证据表明提升可读性是否能改善客观调试性能，也不清楚解释风格应如何与程序员技能水平相匹配。

Method: 采用多阶段众包研究设计，N=103名参与者。首先通过自定义能力评估对参与者进行技能水平分类，然后对比标准解释器消息与两种LLM生成风格（实用型-面向行动，和支架型-脚手架式解释）。测量包括客观调试指标（修复率、尝试次数、修复时间）和主观感知指标（可读性、认知负荷、语气）。

Result: LLM重写的错误消息显著改善了主观评价，其中实用型消息被认为更清晰且认知负担更低，但主观感知的提升并未转化为客观调试性能的统计显著改善。

Conclusion: 揭示了人-AI互补性差距：用户感觉更好的解释不一定使其成为更有效的调试者。建议未来的自适应AI反馈系统应从静态的按技能重写转向基于用户实时修复轨迹的动态调整。

Abstract: Programming error messages are critical for software development, yet they remain difficult for novice programmers to interpret. While Large Language Models (LLMs) can rewrite these errors into clearer explanations, it remains unclear whether increased readability improves objective debugging performance or how explanation styles should align with programmer skill. We present a multi-stage crowdsourced study N=103 evaluating skill-targeted, LLM-generated Python error messages. Using a custom proficiency assessment, we categorized participants by skill level and tested standard interpreter messages against two LLM-generated styles: pragmatic (action-oriented) and contingent (scaffolded explanations). We measured both objective debugging metrics (fix rate, attempts, time-to-fix) and subjective perceptions (readability, cognitive load, tone). Our results show that while LLM-rewritten messages significantly improved subjective evaluations, with pragmatic messages rated as clearer and less cognitively demanding, these perceived gains did not translate into statistically significant improvements in objective debugging performance. This highlights a critical human-AI complementarity gap: explanations that feel better to users do not necessarily make them more effective debuggers. We discuss design implications for adaptive AI feedback systems, arguing that future tools should pivot from static skill-targeted rewriting toward dynamic adjustments based on a user's real-time repair trajectory.

</details>


### [278] [Generation of Web Apps with Agentic IDEs: An Empirical Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20903)
*Manuel Marceca,Maria Teresa Rossi,Leonardo Mariani*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对三种主流智能IDE（Copilot、Cursor和Windsurf）在从零生成五个全栈Web应用方面的能力进行了严格对比分析。结果显示，这些工具在生成成熟模式（如CRUD操作和认证功能）方面表现出较高成熟度，但在生成不太常见的分布式架构（如任务队列架构）时错误显著增多。总体而言，智能IDE无法完全取代开发人员，但改变了开发者的角色定位，使其转向通过自然语言指令和迭代优化来编排LLM代理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能IDE是软件工程领域最重要的创新之一，旨在通过基于LLM的代理加速应用开发。然而，这些工具在涉及完整应用生成的端到端开发任务中的评估仍然有限，需要填补这一评估空白。

Method: 对三种流行的智能IDE（Copilot、Cursor和Windsurf）进行严格的比较分析，任务是从零开始生成五个全栈Web应用程序，涵盖不同类型的架构模式。

Result: 在生成成熟模式（如CRUD操作和认证功能）时表现出较高的成熟度；而在生成较少见的分布式架构（如任务队列架构）时错误显著增多。各智能IDE虽有各自特点，但整体差异较小。

Conclusion: 智能IDE不能取代开发者，但将开发者的角色转变为通过自然语言指令和迭代细化来编排LLM代理。每个智能IDE都各具特色，但整体性能差异不大。

Abstract: Agentic IDEs are among the most significant innovations in software engineering, aiming to accelerate application development through LLM-based agents that can assist developers during development. However, their evaluation in end-to-end development tasks involving the generation of complete applications remains limited. To fill this gap, we propose a rigorous comparative analysis of three popular agentic IDEs (Copilot, Cursor, and Windsurf) in the generation of five full-stack Web applications from scratch.
  Results show high maturity in the generation of established patterns, such as CRUD operations and authentication features. In contrast, the generation of less common distributed architectures, such as a task queue architecture, produces significantly more errors. Overall, results show that Agentic IDEs cannot replace developers but shift their role toward building software by orchestrating LLM-based agents through natural-language instructions and iterative refinement. Yet, each agentic IDE shows its peculiarities, although differences are narrow.

</details>


### [279] [Spike-Killer: Evidence-Gated LLM Assistance for Safe Performance Diagnosis on a Real Windows Workstation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21069)
*Baocheng Zeng,Jinhao Yang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍Spike-Killer，一个人类审批的LLM辅助智能体工作流，用于在真实Windows工作站上诊断帧时间问题。工作流将每个操作视为证据门控的事务，包括记录目标状态、风险分类、快照保存、后置条件验证等。通过Counter-Strike 2的当日研究验证了该模式，获得了高质量的GPU跟踪数据，但明确限定其贡献在于可审计的人机协作模式而非性能结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM辅助智能体在系统诊断中具有灵活性，但精确性不足可能导致不当操作或侵入性采集，带来运营风险。需要一种可审计、人机协作的工作流来确保智能体操作的可靠性和安全性。

Method: 提出证据门控事务工作流：每一步操作都记录目标状态、分类风险、保存快照、验证后置条件，并将失败测量作为一等证据保留。在真实Windows工作站上以Counter-Strike 2为测试应用进行同日实验，使用Windows Performance Recorder采集GPU跟踪数据。

Result: 获得了两个CS2本地Bot GPU跟踪（90.69和85.85秒），均归因于cs2.exe进程，暴露了DxgKrnl Present元数据，且无遗失ETW缓冲区或事件。但研究未报告帧间隔、P99估计或干预效果，仅证明了跟踪完整性。

Conclusion: 该研究的贡献是可审计的人机协作模式，为真实工作站上的可信智能体辅助提供参考，包括在证据不足时设置明确的停止条件，而非性能诊断结论本身。

Abstract: LLM-assisted agents can synthesize system evidence, propose configuration changes, and automate diagnostic tasks, but their flexibility makes an imprecise action or an intrusive collector an operational risk. We present Spike-Killer, a human-approved workflow for diagnosing frame-time complaints on one real Windows workstation. The workflow treats each action as an evidence-gated transaction: it records the exact target state, classifies risk, preserves a snapshot, verifies a postcondition, and retains failed measurements as first-class evidence.
  This experience paper reports a completed same-day study with Counter-Strike 2 as a demanding target application. The evidence bundle contains preserved state snapshots, exploratory microbenchmarks, a ten-run same-state repeatability probe, live telemetry, a repaired over-broad registry action, incompatible presentation-capture attempts, an invalid local replay, and a system-level tracing replacement. Windows Performance Recorder produced two CS2 local-Bot GPU traces of 90.69 and 85.85 seconds; both were attributed to cs2.exe, exposed DxgKrnl Present metadata, and had zero lost ETW buffers or events. These results qualify trace integrity, not performance: the study reports no frame intervals, P99 estimate, or intervention effect. The contribution is an auditable, human-in-the-loop pattern for trustworthy agent assistance on a real workstation, including explicit stop conditions when evidence is insufficient.

</details>


### [280] [Trustworthy RAG: An Evaluation Agent for Detecting Misinformation and Knowledge Poisoning in Generative AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21095)
*Balkrishna Giri,Md Toufique Hasan,Jussi Rasku,Muhammad Waseem,Pekka Abrahamsson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于RAG系统的评估智能体中间件，通过NLI事实核查、五信号毒物检测器和信任指数（T=0.4F+0.35C+0.25(1-P)）来弥合RAG检索内容的安全-可靠性鸿沟，在TruthfulQA上达到91%准确率和100%精确率，并可有效阻止指令注入攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统通常盲目信任检索结果，高语义相关性并不保证事实真实性，攻击者可通过知识投毒插入恶意文档造成定向错误信息，存在安全-可靠性鸿沟。

Method: 提出一个评估智能体中间件，结合三种机制：NLI事实核查、包含五个检测信号的毒物检测器（采用相关性加权聚合）、以及综合信任指数T=0.4F+0.35C+0.25(1-P)，并对高污染场景使用非线性阻尼器。

Result: 在TruthfulQA上使用Llama 3.3 70B达到91%准确率和100%精确率，指令注入召回率达100%；跨三个LLM信任指数的ROC-AUC为0.73-0.81；安全编码场景中阻止不安全建议的指令注入F1达92%；但实体交换等原位编辑和微妙语义削弱仍难以检测。

Conclusion: 该评估智能体能有效检测生成前的有毒上下文，尤其是在指令注入场景下表现优异；但跨数据集泛化需要领域特定校准，且评估的是对有毒上下文的检测而非LLM是否采纳错误信息；代码和实验工件已开源。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) grounds Large Language Model (LLM) outputs in external knowledge, but RAG systems usually trust whatever they retrieve, creating a Security-Reliability Gap: high semantic relevance does not guarantee factual truth. Adversaries exploit this through knowledge poisoning, inserting malicious documents to cause targeted misinformation. We propose an Evaluation Agent, middleware that combines Natural Language Inference (NLI) factual verification, a five-signal poison detector with relevance-weighted aggregation, and a Trust Index T = 0.4 F + 0.35 C + 0.25 (1 - P ) with a non-linear dampener for high-contamination contexts. On TruthfulQA with Llama 3.3 70B, the agent reaches 91% accuracy and 100% precision, with 100% recall on instruction injection, while in-place edits, such as entity swaps, remain hard to detect. Across three LLMs the Trust Index stays discriminative, with a Receiver Operating Characteristic Area Under the Curve (ROC-AUC) of 0.73 to 0.81; generation style matters more than model size, and per-LLM threshold calibration restores baseline competitive accuracy, whereas a weaker FEVER result shows that cross-dataset generalization requires domain-specific calibration. In a software-engineering use case, a secure-coding assistant over guidance from the Open Worldwide Application Security Project (OWASP) Top 10 and the Common Weakness Enumeration (CWE), the agent reliably blocks instruction injection of unsafe advice (F1 92%), while contradiction and subtle semantic weakening remain hard. Throughout, the agent measures detection of poisoned context before generation, not whether the LLM adopts the injected misinformation. We release the proposed approach, attack generator, and experimental artifacts at the link: https://github.com/GPT-Laboratory/TrustworthyRAG.

</details>


### [281] [On the Time and Frequency Domain Representations of Signals for CPS Specification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21167)
*Claudio Mandrioli,Drishti Yadav,Domenico Bianculli*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究使用时频表示来规范网络物理系统（CPS）的验证与验证需求。作者提出了信号-频谱时序逻辑（S2TL），一种能够对频率区间和频率分量间关系进行断言的规范语言，并实现了相应的监控器。研究比较了时域和时频域规范在适用性、表达保真度和噪声容忍度方面的差异，发现时频规范在多种场景下表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大多数规范语言使用信号的时间域表示，这并不总是适合描述信号形状和动态行为。而控制与机器人领域使用频率域表示来表征这些行为。时频表示结合了两个域的能力，因此作者希望探索使用时频表示来规范CPS需求。

Method: 作者首先分析现有CPS需求分类法，识别出能受益于时频表示的需求类别；然后推导出使用时频表示的规范语言的设计需求；提出信号-频谱时序逻辑（S2TL）语言，其支持对频率区间和频率分量间关系的断言；为S2TL语义实现CPS轨迹监控器；为已识别的需求类别定义规范模板，并在适用性、表达保真度和噪声容忍度方面比较时域和时频域公式化表达。

Result: 时域规范仅适用于包含阶跃变化或使用恒定插值的输入轨迹，而时频规范可将评估扩展到通过线性插值生成的轨迹，并提高了对偏移和高频噪声的容忍度，同时实现了与预期系统属性相当的保真度。

Conclusion: 时频表示规范语言（S2TL）能够克服时域规范在适用性和噪声鲁棒性方面的局限性，为CPS需求规范提供了一种更有效的方法，特别是在处理动态行为和信号形状描述方面具有优势。

Abstract: Specification languages are instrumental to the Verification \& Validation of Cyber-Physical Systems (CPSs). Most state-of-the-art specification languages use the time-domain representation of signals, which is not always suitable for describing signal shapes and dynamic behaviours. Instead, fields like control and robotics use the frequency-domain representation to characterise these behaviours. Time-frequency representations combine the capabilities of both domains. We investigate the use of time-frequency representations to specify CPS requirements. We analyse existing taxonomies of CPS requirements to identify which requirement classes can benefit from time-frequency representations. We derive the desiderata for a specification language that uses time-frequency representations and propose Signal-Spectrum Temporal Logic (S2TL), a language enabling assertions over frequency intervals and relations between frequency components. We operationalise the S2TL semantics for monitoring CPS traces, and implement a monitor. We define specification templates for the identified requirement classes and compare time- and time-frequency-domain formulations in terms of applicability, expression fidelity, and noise tolerance of monitoring. We observe that, while time-domain specifications are applicable only to input traces containing step-like changes or using constant interpolation, time-frequency specifications extend their evaluation to traces generated through linear interpolation, and improve tolerance to offset and high-frequency noise, while achieving comparable fidelity to the intended system properties.

</details>


### [282] [Specification Portability Across LLM Development Agents: Cross-Agent Compatibility in Specification-Driven Software Migration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21208)
*Oleg Grynets,Oleksii Ilchuk,Dariia Zatulna,Vasyl Lyashkevych*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了跨Agent规范可移植性问题，通过Oracle到PostgreSQL迁移作为受控软件转换任务，发现规范尺寸不能预测实现质量，跨Agent迁移会导致显著的Agent依赖性退化，并建议在异构多Agent软件工程中不应将规范视为Agent中立的工件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在异构SDD（规范驱动开发）工作流中，不同AI Agent生成的规范可能被其他Agent消费，但规范是否具有跨Agent可移植性、是否会因Agent差异导致质量退化，缺乏系统性研究。

Method: 两阶段实验：第一阶段在1,006个PL/SQL文件上评估规范优先迁移流水线（623个成功再生成，380个在PostgreSQL 16中成功执行）；第二阶段在1,802个Oracle脚本数据集上，使用Amazon Kiro、Google Gemini、GitHub Copilot进行跨Agent实验，以Claude Code和Cursor作为初始单Agent评估补充。使用Token F1、精确匹配、SQL语法有效性、AST精确匹配、AST平均相似度和即时可运行性等指标评估原生与外来规范。

Result: 规范尺寸不能预测实现质量；跨Agent转移会产生显著的Agent依赖性退化。最强退化案例为Gemini直接消费Kiro来源规范时，Token F1仅0.035，SQL语法有效性2.33%，AST平均相似度0.015。重写显著改善Gemini；压缩未提供普遍好处；检索增强摄入是Gemini和Copilot两者的Pareto前沿上唯一共同的策略。

Conclusion: 异构SDD工作流中的规范不应被自动视为Agent中立工件，需要明确考虑规范可移植性、Agent特定解释和基于检索的访问方式。

Abstract: This paper investigates cross-agent specification portability using Oracle-to-PostgreSQL migration as a controlled software transformation task. The study combines two experimental stages. First, a specification-first migration pipeline was evaluated on 1,006 PL/SQL files, of which 623 were successfully regenerated and 380 generated scripts executed successfully in PostgreSQL 16. Second, cross-agent experiments were conducted on a dataset of 1,802 Oracle scripts with corresponding PostgreSQL implementations using Amazon Kiro, Google Gemini, and GitHub Copilot, with Claude Code and Cursor included in the initial single-agent evaluation. Native and foreign specifications were assessed using Token F1, exact match, SQL syntax validity, AST exact match, AST mean similarity, and immediate runnability. The results show that specification size alone does not predict implementation quality and that cross-agent transfer can produce substantial agent-dependent degradation. The strongest replicated case occurred when Gemini directly consumed a Kiro-origin specification, producing a Token F1 of 0.035, SQL syntax validity of 2.33%, and AST mean similarity of 0.015. Rewriting substantially improved Gemini in the tested configuration, compression did not provide a universal benefit, and retrieval-augmented ingestion was the only common strategy represented on the per-agent Pareto frontiers of both Gemini and Copilot. The findings suggest that specifications in heterogeneous SDD workflows should not automatically be treated as agent-neutral artifacts and motivate explicit consideration of specification portability, agent-specific interpretation, and retrieval-based access in multi-agent software engineering.

</details>


### [283] [The Substitution Escrow Threshold: When "Compatible With" Becomes Safe Enough to Buy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21221)
*Amadeus Brandes*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出"替代托管阈值"（Substitution Escrow Threshold）框架，通过五个条件评估基础设施兼容性声明是否真正降低机构风险，而非仅仅降低首次集成成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业基础设施采购中充斥着"S3兼容"、"PostgreSQL兼容"等兼容性声明，这些声明通常被当作未来替换选项的代理指标，但大多数兼容性声明并未真正托管其所暗示的替代路径，导致采购方可能高估兼容性带来的风险降低。

Method: 提出包含五个条件的替代托管阈值框架：边界闭合（boundary closure）、可执行一致性（executable conformance）、托管独立性（custody independence）、状态与操作可逆性（state and operations reversibility）、扩展隔离（extension quarantine）。将该框架应用于五个基础设施案例（OCI、Kubernetes、OpenTelemetry、S3、PostgreSQL），映射到五个不同的结果单元。

Result: 框架产出了可用于企业架构师、平台工程师、采购团队和投资者的可操作诊断工具，并将AI基础设施确定为该框架最紧迫的下一应用领域。

Conclusion: 兼容性声明需要系统化评估其真实替代价值，本文提供的五条件框架能够区分"真正降低机构风险"与"仅降低首次集成成本"的兼容性声明，并为相关决策者提供诊断方法。

Abstract: Enterprise infrastructure buyers routinely evaluate compatibility claims--"S3-compatible," "PostgreSQL-compatible," "OpenAI compatible"--as proxies for future substitution options. Yet most compatibility claims do not escrow the substitution path they imply. This paper introduces the Substitution Escrow Threshold, a five-condition framework that determines when a compatibility claim genuinely reduces institutional risk versus merely reducing first-integration cost. The five conditions--boundary closure, executable conformance, custody independence, state and operations reversibility, and extension quarantine--are applied to five infrastructure cases (OCI, Kubernetes, OpenTelemetry, S3, PostgreSQL) that populate five distinct outcome cells. The framework produces actionable diagnostics for enterprise architects, platform engineers, procurement teams, and investors evaluating compatibility-dependent infrastructure decisions, and identifies AI infrastructure as the framework's most urgent next application.

</details>


### [284] [Human-AI Collaboration in Requirements Engineering: Evidence of the Negative Effect of LLMs on Requirements Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21298)
*Giovanna Broccia,Julian Frattini,Chetan Arora,Maurice H. ter Beek,Alessandro Fantechi,Andreas Vogelsang,Alessio Ferrari*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过34名参与者的交叉对照实验，研究了LLM辅助对人工需求检查（RI）的影响。结果表明LLM支持对气味检测准确性有负面影响，对分类和任务时长无显著影响，且从开始就使用LLM学习RI可能减缓技能习得。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然LLMs在需求工程任务中的潜力引起了广泛兴趣，但关于LLM作为人类RI协作助手的实证证据仍然稀缺，需要填补这一研究空白。

Method: 采用34名参与者的受控交叉设计实验，参与者在有无LLM支持下检查文本规格说明，识别和分类需求气味并记录检查时间。使用每项结果变量一个贝叶斯回归模型进行数据分析，同时考虑交叉设计带来的效度威胁以及协变量和中介变量。

Result: LLM支持对气味检测准确性产生负面影响，但对气味分类和任务持续时间没有显著影响。实验期间存在学习效应，但当首次检查以LLM支持进行时，学习效应减弱。

Conclusion: LLM支持不必然改善性能，对新手检查者可能反而产生阻碍；从开始就使用LLM支持学习RI可能减缓技能习得过程，对LLM支持的学-习构成潜在威胁。

Abstract: Background. Requirements inspection (RI) is a well-established practice for detecting potential defects in requirements artifacts early in the software lifecycle. Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have stimulated interest in their potential to support requirements engineering (RE) tasks. However, empirical evidence on the effects of LLMs when used as collaborative assistants in human-performed RI remains scarce. Aims. We aim to investigate the impact of LLM support on human-performed RI, considering inspection effectiveness in terms of smell identification and severity classification (i.e., nocuous vs innocuous), as well as inspection duration. Method. We conducted a controlled crossover design experiment with 34 participants, who inspected textual specifications with and without LLM support, identifying and classifying requirements smells while recording inspection time. We analyzed the data using one Bayesian regression model per outcome variable, accounting for validity threats induced by the crossover design as well as covariates and mediators. Results. Results show that LLM support negatively affects smell detection accuracy but has no significant effect on smell classification or task duration. A learning effect is present across experimental periods, but reduced when RI is first performed with LLM support. Conclusions. Our findings provide empirical evidence that LLM support does not necessarily improve performance and may, instead, hinder it for novice inspectors. Moreover, the results suggest that learning RI with LLM-support from the beginning may slow down the skill acquisition process, implying threats for LLM-supported learning.

</details>


### [285] [Beyond Fault Localization: A Trajectory-Level Study of LLM Agents for Microservice Root Cause Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21310)
*Qisheng Lu,Aoyang Fang,Junjielong Xu,Jin'ao Shang,Songhan Zhang,Yifan Yang,Xiaochuan Yan,Pinjia He*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出将微服务自动化根因分析（RCA）视为可观测的诊断过程，构建轨迹级评估框架，分析3500条诊断轨迹后发现答案正确性与诊断质量之间存在脱节，并据此提出DiagGuard两阶段防御架构，将Acc@1从43.5%提升至52.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RCA评估仅关注端点正确性（是否定位到责任服务），无法揭示诊断的证据基础或故障传播路径，而这正是值班站点可靠性工程师判断是否需要采取行动的关键信息。

Method: 提出轨迹级评估框架，将RCA视为可观测的诊断过程，针对人工整理的故障传播路径评估智能体执行过程；分析3500条诊断轨迹以刻画智能体的调查行为和遥测数据使用方式；将诊断失败原因归类为三类（证据遗漏、证据误读、无端推断），并据此设计DiagGuard两阶段防御架构：grounding阶段在定位前调查可用观测，verification阶段审计诊断与观测的一致性。

Result: 发现答案正确性与诊断质量之间存在脱节：智能体可能正确定位故障源但无法重建传播路径。成功调查会停留在故障影响面、基于检索到的证据行动并随搜索深入扩展查询手段；失败源于决定性证据被遗漏、检索证据被误读或用无根据推断替代缺失证据。在独立设置（不同模型、基准和拓扑）下，DiagGuard将Acc@1从43.5%提升至52.5%。

Conclusion: 轨迹级评估能揭示最终答案指标所掩盖的局限性，并为改进自动化RCA提供可操作的指导。诊断过程的质量与最终定位正确性应被同等重视，DiagGuard通过grounding与verification两阶段审计有效提升了诊断可靠性。

Abstract: Existing evaluations of automated root cause analysis (RCA) for microservices assess diagnostic performance mainly by endpoint correctness: whether a method localizes the responsible service. This criterion enables comparison but does not reveal the evidentiary basis of a diagnosis or the fault-propagation route connecting the source to observed symptoms, both of which an on-call site reliability engineer needs to judge whether action is warranted. We therefore treat RCA as an observable diagnostic process. Our trajectory-level framework evaluates agent executions against manually curated service-level fault-propagation paths. Applied to a public microservice RCA benchmark, it analyzes 3,500 diagnostic trajectories, characterizing where agents investigate and how they use retrieved telemetry. We find a disconnect between answer correctness and diagnostic quality: an agent may localize the fault source yet fail to reconstruct its propagation. Successful investigations stay on the fault-impact surface, act on retrieved evidence, and broaden their query repertoire as the search deepens. Failures arise when decisive evidence is omitted, retrieved evidence is misinterpreted, or unsupported inference substitutes for missing evidence. We operationalize this taxonomy as DiagGuard, a two-stage defense-in-depth architecture in which grounding surveys available observations before localization and verification audits the diagnosis against them. In an independent setting with a different model, benchmark, and service topology, DiagGuard raises Acc@1 from 43.5% to 52.5%. These results show that trajectory-level evaluation exposes limitations hidden by final-answer metrics and provides actionable guidance for improving automated RCA.

</details>


### [286] [AI-to-AI Code Reviews of GitHub Pull Requests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21311)
*Niruthiha Selvanayagam,Taher A. Ghaleb*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个大规模AI对AI代码审查数据集，通过关联AI属性PR与AI审查事件（来源于CodAGE数据集），分析了248,641个经受AI审查的AI生成PR。研究发现跨产品AI对AI审查在2025年Q1至Q3期间增长了超过两个数量级，审查输出因作者-审查者配置而异，且同产品组中双角色审查者的平均评论数更高（58-65%），但效应量较小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编程代理正越来越多地参与到PR流程的两端（AI作者和AI审查者），形成了AI审查AI的闭环。然而，目前缺乏对这一现象的大规模实证研究，因此需要构建数据集并量化这种AI到AI审查的模式、趋势和特征。

Method: 从公开的CodAGE数据集中，将AI属性PR与AI属性审查事件进行关联，构建了包含248,641个至少收到一次AI审查的独立AI属性PR数据集。按跨产品（不同AI产品间）和同产品（同一AI产品内）进行分类，并比较不同审查者（如CodeRabbit、Copilot）的评论行为。

Result: 跨产品AI审查约占识别出的代理编写PR的1.6%，但从2025年Q1到Q3绝对量增长了超过两个数量级。CodeRabbit对Claude Code编写PR标注35%的重构评论，对比Copilot编写PR仅10.5%。在四个双角色审查者中，三个的同产品组平均每人PR评论数高出58-65%，但效应量小。跨产品对的中位延迟为1.2分钟，同产品对为4.7分钟。

Conclusion: 闭环AI对AI审查正在增长但仍属少数活动，审查输出因作者代理组和产品配置而异。研究揭示了AI审查生态系统的多样性，但也指出时间戳可用性和审查者构成的差异可能限制某些比较的可靠性。

Abstract: AI coding agents are increasingly integrated into software development workflows, operating on both sides of the pull-request (PR) process: AI authoring agents create or modify PRs, while AI reviewers evaluate them. This creates a closed loop in which one AI coding agent reviews a contribution attributed to another. We construct a large-scale dataset of AI-to-AI code review by linking AI-attributed PRs with AI-attributed review events from CodAGE, a public dataset of coding-agent-generated GitHub events. Our dataset contains 248,641 unique AI-attributed PRs that received at least one AI-attributed review. Of these, 45,269 received cross-product review and 208,145 received same-product review; 4,773 PRs received both. Cross-product AI-to-AI review occurred in approximately 1.6% of identified agent-authored PRs but was substantial in absolute terms, and its volume increased by more than two orders of magnitude from 2025-Q1 to 2025-Q3. Reviewer output varied across author-reviewer configurations. CodeRabbit labeled 35.0% of its comments on Claude Code-authored PRs as refactor comments, compared with 10.5% on Copilot-authored PRs, although this difference may reflect characteristics of the PRs rather than the reviewer. For three of four dual-role reviewers, mean comments per PR were 58-65% higher in the same-product group, although effect sizes were small or negligible and the difference was concentrated in the upper tail. Among pairs with complete, nonnegative timestamps, the observed median latency was 1.2 minutes for cross-product pairs and 4.7 minutes for same-product pairs; differential timestamp availability and reviewer composition limit this comparison. Overall, closed-loop AI-to-AI review is increasing but remains a minority of identified agent activity, with review output varying across authoring-agent groups and product configurations.

</details>


### [287] [Natural-Language Workflows Are Not Software Yet: Artifact-Driven Compilation for Reliable Agent Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21341)
*Xiangzhe Xu,Hanxi Guo,Guangyu Shen,Siyuan Cheng,Xiangyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Artic，一种工件驱动的工作流编译器，将自然语言工作流转化为工件驱动形式，通过显式声明数据依赖、约束门控和显式控制转移来提高智能体执行工作流的可靠性和一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言工作流为智能体提供了软件式接口，但执行并不可靠：工作流描述常隐含数据依赖，智能体在高上下文压力下难以遵循长流程或分支指令。

Method: Artic将自然语言工作流转换为工件驱动形式，每一步声明读写的工件，用约束门控生成产物，并通过显式控制转移路由执行。编译器识别依赖过多状态或包含复杂控制逻辑的步骤，并通过约束优化进行细化。忠实性检查被分解为局部义务，并使用场景化试运行验证编译后工作流区域与源工作流的一致性。

Result: 在来自11个真实领域工作流的488个问题实例上评估，Artic相比原始文本工作流将任务解析率提高了28个百分点；编译后的工作流在跨模型和重复执行设置下分别提高了32和56个百分点的一致性。

Conclusion: 工件驱动的工作流编译能显著提升智能体执行自然语言工作流的可靠性与一致性，为领域专家编写可复用程序提供了更可靠的执行基础。

Abstract: Natural-language workflows offer a software-like interface for agents: domain experts can write reusable procedures, and agents can execute them as instructions. This promise is not yet reliable. Workflow descriptions often leave data dependencies implicit, so the executor must infer which prior results a step should use; agents can also fail to follow long or branching instructions under context pressure. We propose Artic, an artifact-driven workflow compiler that transforms a natural-language workflow into an artifact-driven workflow in which each step declares the artifacts it reads and writes, constraints gate produced artifacts, and explicit control transfers route execution. This representation exposes the enforcement burden placed on agent execution, allowing the compiler to identify steps that depend on too much state or contain difficult control logic and refine them through constrained optimization. To validate the LLM-assisted transformation, Artic decomposes faithfulness checking into local obligations and uses scenario-based dry runs to test whether compiled workflow regions conform to the source workflow. We evaluate Artic on 488 problem instances from 11 real-world domain workflows; it improves task resolve rate by 28 percentage points over the original text workflow. We also show that workflows compiled by Artic are 32 and 56 percentage points more consistent in cross-model and repeated-execution setups, respectively.

</details>


### [288] [AI with Authority, from Application to Silicon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21356)
*Jason Hickey*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文报告称生成式AI彻底改变了机器验证的成本结构：过去60年机器验证成本高昂，如今在AI速度下不仅经济且对生产力至关重要。一位研究者在五周内仅凭消费级AI订阅，指挥一小队AI智能体从应用代码、经验证的编译器到RISC-V处理器完成流片，全程无人审阅证明、无人类编写RTL。核心方法"Salt方法"依赖证明内核作为不可伪造的裁判。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去六十年机器验证成本高昂，仅适用于特殊工件。研究者希望探索生成式AI能否反转这一关系——让机器验证从昂贵负担变为必要且经济的基础设施，从而让一人安全地大规模指挥自主机器工作。

Method: 提出"Salt方法"：以证明内核为核心纪律，数学声明以内核验证的工件形式在AI代理间传递；人类注意力仅保留给语句、设计和裁决。验证链条从Lean 4内核逐环节贯通到SAT检查的硅边界等价性，并发布完整账目：定理溯源、预注册token计量、人类时间下限、错误台账（编号至#256）。

Result: 一位研究者在五周内用消费级AI订阅，指挥AI代理完成从应用代码到经过验证的编译器与执行器、再到RISC-V处理器流片的完整流程，在社区硅穿梭计划上完成流片。在零错误证明到达记录的前提下，错误台账计数达到#256（其中#79从未分配，后续捕获以无编号记录）。

Conclusion: 生成式AI使机器验证从仅适用于特殊工件的昂贵开销变为日常生产力必需：证明内核作为不可被幻觉证明通过的裁判，使得一人即可安全地大规模指挥自主机器工作，验证链全链路可审计，且以完整公开账目支撑其可信度。

Abstract: For sixty years, machine verification has been a major cost overhead, affordable only for exceptional artifacts. Here we report that generative AI inverts this relationship: at AI speed, machine verification is not only economical but essential to productivity --- it is the incorruptible referee that lets one person safely direct autonomous machine work at scale. In five weeks, one researcher on consumer AI subscriptions directed a small fleet of AI agents from application code, through a verified compiler and executive, to a RISC-V processor taped out on a community silicon shuttle; no proof passed through human review, and no RTL was written by a human. The working discipline --- the Salt method --- rests on a proof kernel no hallucinated proof can pass: mathematical claims travel between agents as kernel-checked artifacts, and human attention is reserved for statements, designs, and rulings. Verification is stated link by link, from the Lean 4 kernel to SAT-checked equivalence at the silicon boundary. We publish the complete accounting: theorem provenance, a pre-registered token meter, floor-bounded human time, and an error ledger whose catch numbering runs to #256 --- a monotone counter over the mathematics campaign's append-only flags ledger, maintained 2026-07-07 to 2026-07-20 (one number, #79, was never assigned; later catches are recorded un-numbered) --- against zero incorrect proofs reaching the record.

</details>
